====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If the policy owner stops paying premiums after three years and elects Reduced Paid-Up Insurance, what happens?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: Coverage continues for a reduced death benefit with no further premiums required; the amount is based on the cash value and the insured's attained age.
EVIDENCE: 9.2 REDUCED PAID-UP INSURANCE: Continue coverage for a reduced death benefit with no further premiums required. Amount based on cash value and insured's attained age.
====================================================================================================
